The first, second and sixth were called `AMetricBidPersistsTheMetricTypeDefaultsForEveryStyleChange`, `AnImperialBidKeepsTheWindowsTableForEveryStyleChange` and `CondM08_UnsupportedType.ASaveRewritesTheTypeWithTheMetricDefaultsInAMetricBid` until job 5. Job 5 added the suite `CondStyle02_FieldWins.*` (12 tests, :548 to :878; W1 to W6 are ports of the Windows class `EditConditionTypeChangeAndElevationTests`) and renamed `CondStyle02_Spacing.AStyleChangePersistsTheTypeDefaultSpacingUnlessEdited` to `AStyleChangeKeepsTheSpacingTheFieldShows` (:223).
- **Failing first** (2026-11-19): with the metric parameter added and the old behaviour kept, the Conditions area gave 163 tests, 159 passed, 4 failed (for example `CHECK_EQ(type_change_defaults(Linear, true).spacing, 3.937007874015748)` failed with 4 != 3.937007874015748); the imperial matrix test passed as a regression guard.
- **Residual risk and device check** (updated 2026-11-20, job 5): the Edit Condition screen in a metric and in an imperial bid was not seen on a device (DEV-03). After a style change the Spacing field keeps its old text and Save writes what the field shows; the Linear / Area thickness is the field, Count and Attachment take the table thickness. COND-M08 (a stored unsupported condition type such as 4) was unverified on Windows in job 5 (**Superseded / updated 2026-11-21 (job 6):** decided and tested, the editor keeps the stored type and shows a disabled "Unknown (N)" entry, see the section for job 6; owner note e is resolved): the iOS tests then pinned only the type change and the unchanged spacing and no thickness (owner note e).
- **Superseded / updated 2026-11-20 (job 5)**: Windows `b9902e6` fixed both Windows behaviours that the deviation avoided (the type default thickness is metric-aware, `ost_visualizer/presentation/dialogs/edit_condition_dialog.py:1236-1239`, used at `:1458-1463`) and the spacing rule of decision (a) of 2026-11-16 is replaced as well: on a type change Windows writes the thickness and the spacing with the field values (`:1559`, `:1589`, `:1624`: `if type_changed or ...`), so a retyped value equal to the stored one survives, and the Spacing text is reset only when the dialog is first populated (`:1278-1280`). iOS follows: `condition_to_save` saves the Spacing and the Linear / Area thickness as the fields show them, also when equal to the stored value; only the Count / Attachment thickness (metric aware: 100 mm and 0 in a metric bid, 4.0 and 0.0 in an imperial bid) and the backout come from the table; `TypeChangeDefaults` lost its spacing member (`StyleDimensionDefaults.spacing` stays as the mirror of the Windows `TYPE_DEFAULTS`, `:94`, which the dialog reads when it is first populated). The new `condition_changes_for_save(stored, form, metric)` marks Spacing, Thickness and Backout as changed on every type change even when equal to the stored value (literal write parity with Windows `type_changed or ...`; observable only for a stored NULL column) and equals `collect_condition_changes` otherwise; the `.mm` calls it once. The 2026-11-19 "deviation" for metric bids is gone: the metric defaults the dialog shows are Windows behaviour. Status stays fixed-ui-unverified. **Updated 2026-11-21 (job 8):** verified on device: DEV-03 PASS (owner, 2026-11-21, owner's iPhone, IPA of run 44953904172); status fixed (was fixed-ui-unverified); the "was not seen on a device" statement of the residual-risk bullet above is history.

#### Code change 2: COND-ELEV-01, after a Save `is_top` and `z_value` come from the condition name (decided 2026-11-19 as a deviation from the dialog's values; parity since job 5)

- **Decision**: after a Save, `is_top` and `z_value` are derived from the condition name with `extract_z_value_from_name` (`ost_domain::derive_elevation_from_name`; an unreadable name gives 0.0 and false), so the in-memory condition equals what a reload gives. This matches the effective Windows behaviour, which reloads the conditions from the database after every save (`ost_visualizer/application/services/project_write_service.py:1327-1337`); only the dialog's own `is_top` and `z_value` (`edit_condition_dialog.py:1430` at `6d4786f`, `:1454-1457` at `b9902e6`) are no longer used.
- **Files** (updated 2026-11-20, job 5; lines of `ConditionStyleChange.cpp` and `EditConditionViewController.mm` renumbered in job 6): `Core/Domain/PositionParser.hpp:118` and `PositionParser.cpp:1620` (`derive_elevation_from_name`; the elevation reader `elevation_text_inches` is `PositionParser.cpp:1568` since job 5), `Core/Domain/ConditionStyleChange.cpp:185` (renumbered in job 6; `condition_to_save` derives from the saved name) and `.hpp`, `Core/Domain/ConditionFormRules.hpp` (comment), `Core/Persistence/ProjectStore/ProjectStore.cpp:71` (`extract_z_value` wraps the shared function), `Controllers/EditConditionViewController/EditConditionViewController.mm:611-614` (renumbered in job 6; the text is validated and refused as before, but not stored), `Config/Tests/Conditions/test_condition_elevation.cpp` (new on 2026-11-19), `Config/Tests/Conditions/test_condition_form.cpp` (comment only).
- **Tests** (updated 2026-11-20, job 5; 8 new on 2026-11-19, all in `Config/Tests/Conditions/test_condition_elevation.cpp`): `CondElev01_SaveDerivesFromName.TheNamedCasesGiveTheWindowsReload` (:147), `CondElev01_SaveDerivesFromName.TopSegmentWithAnEmptyElevationIsNotTop` (:173), `CondElev01_SaveDerivesFromName.StaleStoredValuesAreReplacedAndNoFieldIsWritten` (:188), `CondElev01_SaveDerivesFromName.AStyleChangeDerivesFromTheNameToo` (:207), `CondElev01_SaveDerivesFromName.EveryOracleRowGivesTheWindowsReload` (:222), `CondElev01_SharedDerivation.TheLoaderAndTheSaveGiveTheSameValueOnEveryRow` (:249), `CondElev01_SharedDerivation.AnUnreadableNameIsZeroAndNotTopWhateverTheOutputsHeld` (:275), `CondElev01_ReloadParity.TheSavedInMemoryConditionEqualsTheReloadedOne` (:298).
- **Failing first** (2026-11-19): the new tests failed on assertions before the change (for example the round trip through the real store: 244 of 261 conditions differed between memory and reload).
- **Residual risk and device check** (updated 2026-11-20, job 5): the dialog, save and 3D redraw path was not run on a device (DEV-04; DEV-10 for the closing-inch-mark rule of job 6). The 2026-11-19 risk (an elevation text that the loader does not parse, for example a metric "3200" without a unit or decimal inches such as `10' 6.3"`, reloaded as 0.0 and not top) no longer exists: the dialog now refuses those texts.
- **Superseded / updated 2026-11-20 (job 5)**: Windows `b9902e6` unified the two parsers: `ost_visualizer/domain/services/elevation_text.py:131-144` (`elevation_text_inches`; the helpers moved there byte for byte from `position_parser.py`, which now only delegates: `extract_z_value_from_name` at `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/parsers/position_parser.py:23-33`), the dialog validates with it (`edit_condition_dialog.py:1423-1447`: an unreadable text or an infinite value is refused with the title "Invalid Value" and the message `"<text>" is not a valid elevation. <hint>`) and the placeholder is `e.g. 3.2 m` in a metric bid (`:386-389`). iOS follows with the same Core parser: `elevation_text_inches` (`Core/Domain/PositionParser.cpp:1568`; status Value, NotAnElevation or Raises) serves `extract_z_value_from_name` (`PositionParser.cpp:1594`) and `parse_elevation_z` (`Core/Domain/ConditionFormRules.cpp:46`), which refuses a bare number, mm, mixed units, `5'` alone and a zero denominator with the Windows message, the hints `Use metres or centimetres, for example "3.2 m" or "320 cm".` (metric) and `Use feet and inches with unit marks, for example 10' 6".` (imperial; the same texts are accepted in both measurement systems, only the hint and the placeholder depend on the mode) and the title "Invalid Value"; `elevation_placeholder(bool)` (`ConditionFormRules.cpp:104` since job 6) gives `e.g. 3.2 m` / `e.g. 10' 6"` (wired in `EditConditionViewController+TableView.mm:172` since job 6). The 2026-11-19 "deviation" (is_top and z_value from the saved name) is now plain parity except for a blank elevation with Top (read from the code, not run: the Windows DTO says top with 0.0, iOS says not top, like a reload; Windows reloads after the save anyway). Facts for the owner at `b9902e6` (**Superseded / updated 2026-11-21 (job 6):** Windows `49a2d59` refuses `5'6` and `10'6` in the dialog, see the next bullet; `5'x` is still accepted as 60 inches): Windows accepted `5'6` and `5'x` as 60 inches and read `10'6` as 120 (owner note a, ported as is then); a feet run beyond the float range raises an uncaught `OverflowError` in the Windows dialog (it catches only `ValueError`; run in memory), iOS refuses it with the normal message; an existing condition whose name suffix the loader cannot read (for example `Wall @T 3200`) can now not be saved after any edit until its elevation is fixed or cleared, the same as on Windows (`_apply_changes` validates every dirty save, `:1737-1742` at `b9902e6`, `:1760-1765` at `49a2d59`). Status stays fixed-ui-unverified; the new Objective-C wiring is only the placeholder and the alert.
- **Superseded / updated 2026-11-21 (job 6)**: Windows `49a2d59` added a rule on top of the shared parser: after `elevation_text_inches` accepts the text, the dialog refuses it when there is no `"` anywhere in it, a letter or digit follows a foot mark (U+0027 or U+2032, with or without a space) and the rest is not a lone `x` / `X` (`edit_condition_dialog.py:72`, the elevation block `:1437-1461`, the refusal `:1444-1445`; the loader's `extract_z_value_from_name` is `position_parser.py:21-31` and did not change in logic). iOS follows with `ost_domain::elevation_text_needs_closing_inch_mark` (`Core/Domain/ConditionFormRules.hpp:87`, `.cpp:69`), called by `parse_elevation_z` (`.cpp:62`) after the shared parser, with the same alert; the loader and the Core parser are unchanged (a stored `Footing @T 5'6` still reads 60, top). The facts for the owner of the previous bullet are obsolete for `5'6` and `10'6` (owner note a is resolved); the oddities of the new Windows rule and the consequence for stored names (every dirty save of such a condition is refused until the elevation is fixed or cleared) are under "Possible Windows defects" and in notes k and l. Tests: `CondElev02_ClosingInchMark.*` (14, new file `Config/Tests/Conditions/test_condition_elevation_foot_mark.cpp`); changed: `CondElev01_Validation.AnAcceptedElevationReloadsToTheValueTheDialogSaved`, `CondElev01_Elevation.TheTextGivesZAndNanIsRefused`. Device check DEV-10. Status stays fixed-ui-unverified; no Objective-C line changed. **Updated 2026-11-21 (job 8):** verified on device: DEV-04 and DEV-10 both PASS (owner, 2026-11-21, owner's iPhone, IPA of run 44953904172); status fixed (was fixed-ui-unverified); the "was not run on a device" statement of the residual-risk bullet above is history.
- **Superseded / updated 2026-11-27 (job 9)**: Windows `0a24294` (rule at `ost_visualizer/presentation/dialogs/edit_condition_dialog.py:184-186`, the validation block `:1622-1651`; `ost_visualizer/domain/services/elevation_text.py:5-7`, `:31-39`, `:138-158`; loader `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/parsers/position_parser.py:22-31`) rewrote the rule: the dialog refuses an accepted text when it does not start with a full feet-and-inches value (`IMPERIAL_FEET_INCHES` on the inch-mark-normalised text) and a non-blank character of any script follows a foot mark (a lone `x` may be followed by U+180E, U+200B, U+200C, U+200D, U+2060 or U+FEFF); U+2033 and U+201D are inch marks (`normalize_inch_marks`) in the parser, the dialog AND the loader, so a stored name such as `Wall @T 5'6` followed by U+2033 reads 66 inches (iOS read 60); a non-finite elevation (inches-only, metres, centimetres) is refused in the dialog (the loader gives z 0.0, not top, one warning, neighbours unaffected). Windows tests `tests/presentation/dialogs/test_edit_condition_dialog.py::EditConditionElevationRuleTests` (`:1774`) and `tests/domain/services/test_elevation_text.py::ElevationTextInchMarkAndRangeTests` (`:575`). iOS ported all of it: `elevation_text_needs_closing_inch_mark` (`Core/Domain/ConditionFormRules.cpp:70-107`, called by `parse_elevation_z` at `:62-63`) and `normalize_inch_marks` (`Core/Domain/PositionParser.cpp:1233-1235`, used by `elevation_text_inches` and the gate at `:1575` and `:1581`); the metric hint reads "meters or centimeters" (US spelling of the Windows text of `441b45e` / `d2caf63`). The statement above that the job-6 rule is the Windows rule, the owner decision of job 7 ("stray quote ported as is") and the facts for the owner of the previous two bullets are superseded; the loader still reads a stored `Footing @T 5'6` as 60 inches, top (note l, unchanged). Tests: `CondElev03_WindowsElevationRuleTests.*` (7), `CondElev02_ClosingInchMark.*` (the 189-row table recomputed), `ElevationPort.DoublePrimeAndRightDoubleQuoteAreInchMarks`, `ElevationPort.AValueBeyondTheFloatRangeIsAnUnreadableElevation`, `ElevationTextValue.MetresBeyondTheFloatRangeRaiseLikeAnUnreadableText`, the regenerated `ElevationOracleData.hpp`. Commits `2e804e1` and `b4662a8`. The Objective-C side is unchanged (the alert text comes from Core); device check DEV-04 and DEV-10 passed for the earlier rule, the changed rule has host tests only (P2 checks in `DEVICE_CHECKS.md`).

#### Code change 3: DLG-FINITE-01 (Rise and Run half), a whitespace-only Rise or Run is refused like Windows

- **Decision**: a whitespace-only Rise or Run is refused with "Rise must be a number." / "Run must be a number." like Windows (`ost_visualizer/presentation/dialogs/edit_condition_dialog.py:1561-1566` and `:1573-1578` at `b9902e6`; `:1538-1543` and `:1550-1555` at `6d4786f`); only an EMPTY field is 0. On 2026-11-19 only this half was decided and the condition-number half stayed open; **updated 2026-11-20 (job 5):** Windows answered the condition-number half, see the last bullet.
- **Files** (updated 2026-11-20, job 5; the lines of `ConditionFormRules` and `EditConditionViewController.mm` renumbered in job 6): `Core/Domain/ConditionFormRules.cpp:168` (`parse_slope_value`: only an empty text is blank) and `.hpp:139` (contract), `Controllers/EditConditionViewController/EditConditionViewController.mm:568-569` (comment only: the dialog already handed the raw field text to Core, so the platform behaviour follows from the Core change), `Config/Tests/Conditions/test_condition_form.cpp`.
- **Tests** (2 added, 1 changed; lines updated in job 5): `DlgFinite01_RiseRun.AWhiteSpaceOnlyFieldIsNotANumberLikeWindows` (`Config/Tests/Conditions/test_condition_form.cpp:350`), `DlgFinite01_RiseRun.WhiteSpaceAroundANumberAndTheOtherRefusalsStayAsTheyWere` (`Config/Tests/Conditions/test_condition_form.cpp:396`); `CondVal01_RiseRun.AnEmptyFieldIsZeroAndANumberIsTheNumber` (`Config/Tests/Conditions/test_condition_form.cpp:327`) replaces the former blank-field test whose "   " case pinned the old behaviour.
- **Failing first**: the new whitespace test failed on 15 texts x Rise / Run x Linear / Area before the change (a scratch run with the filter gave 5 tests, 4 passed, 1 failed). **Review**: CONFIRMED-FIXED by an independent reviewer. Mutation: putting back the old strip in parse_slope_value makes the new whitespace test fail on 120 assertions (60 rows) while the empty-field, C0-separator and neighbouring-behavior tests keep passing; the file was restored byte-identically (sha256). Other plausible wrong fixes are caught as well (rejecting empty text, the finite-number wording, stripping around a number). Limit: that UITextField.text returns exactly what was typed or pasted is an assumption; Rise and Run use a decimal pad with no space key, so a whitespace-only value arrives only by paste or an external keyboard.
- **Device check** (updated 2026-11-20, job 5): the Rise and Run half has none of its own (the `.mm` change is a comment, the rule is Core and host-tested); the condition-number alert is DEV-05.
- **Superseded / updated 2026-11-20 (job 5), the condition-number half**: the open question (widen `Condition::ref_no` and the column reads to 64 bits, or keep refusing 2147483648 and up) was answered by Windows `b9902e6`, which caps the condition number at the Access Long limit 2147483647 (`ost_visualizer/application/dtos/update_condition_dto.py:5-9`, `MAX_CONDITION_NUMBER`; the 2026-11-19 statement that Windows accepts any whole number the column holds, up to 64 bits, was wrong). The dialog refuses a larger number with "Condition number must be between 1 and 2147483647." (`edit_condition_dialog.py:1464-1492`, cap at :1483-1490; a number below 1 or text that is not a whole number keeps "Condition number must be a positive whole number.", and a huge negative number gets that positive message because Windows checks `< 1` first). When a renumber would move another condition past the limit, `update_condition` raises `ConditionNumberOverflowError` before it writes anything (`ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/components/condition_operations.py:623-641`: `MAX([RefNo])` of the bid's rows from the new number up, the edited condition excluded, probed only when the new number is taken; re-raised at :719-720), the use case returns its text as the error (`update_condition_use_case.py:44-45`) and the dialog shows it under "Save Failed" (`edit_condition_dialog.py:1787-1794`). Windows tests: `tests/presentation/dialogs/test_edit_condition_dialog.py:450-473`, `tests/infrastructure/mdb/components/test_condition_operations.py:1300-1340` and `:2376-2418`. iOS follows: `parse_condition_number` (`Core/Domain/ConditionFormRules.cpp:109` since job 6) refuses 2147483648 and up, also beyond 64 bits, with the range message and accepts 2147483647; `ost_store::update_condition` (`Core/Persistence/ProjectStore/Components/ProjectStore+Conditions.cpp`, the shift at :136) probes `MAX(RefNo)` with the Windows predicate and, when a non-NULL maximum is at or above 2147483647, rolls the WHOLE save back (nothing is written, also not the other fields) and returns `Condition number N cannot be used: the conditions numbered N and up would move up by one, and one of them is already 2147483647, the highest condition number.` through a new optional `std::string *error` (default nullptr: the 29 existing test call sites of `update_condition` at `275cf66` are unchanged, and the one production call site, `Services/ImportService.mm`, was extended to pass it); the holder of 2147483647 can still move down and a holder in another bid never blocks; the in-memory twin (`Core/Domain/ConditionChanges.hpp`) refuses instead of overflowing; the Objective-C pass-through (`Services/ImportService.mm:437` `updateConditionWithUID:inBid:changes:error:`, the delegate parameter `failureMessage:` at `ViewController+Conditions.mm:162`, `EditConditionViewController.mm:402` (both renumbered in job 6) `_showSaveFailedAlertWithMessage:`) shows the alert titled "Save Failed" with that text and the generic text only when the message is empty. Tests: `CondRef01_ConditionNumber.ANumberAboveTheHighestConditionNumberIsRefusedWithTheRangeMessage` (`test_condition_form.cpp:101`; renamed from `ANumberBeyondTheStorableRangeIsRefused`), `DlgFinite01_ConditionNumber.*` (3: `:124`, `:141`, `:170`), `DlgFinite01_SaveFailed.*` (2: `:548`, `:566`), `DlgFinite01_ConditionNumberLimit.*` (7, `Config/Tests/Conditions/test_condition_update.cpp:821` to `:1008`). Not changed in job 5 (owner note g; **Superseded / updated 2026-11-21 (job 6):** `duplicate_condition` is guarded since job 6 and Windows guards its allocator too, see the next bullet; only `ost_store::shift_condition_ref_nos` is still unguarded): in job 5 `duplicate_condition` still computed `COALESCE(MAX(RefNo),0)+1` and read it with `column_int` (Windows `_allocate_condition_identities`, `condition_operations.py:41-58` at `b9902e6`, was unguarded too), and `ost_store::shift_condition_ref_nos` (no production caller) has no guard. Status fixed -> fixed-ui-unverified (the alert path is Objective-C; device check DEV-05).
- **Superseded / updated 2026-11-21 (job 6), new condition numbers**: Windows `49a2d59` guards the numbers that NEW conditions get (`_allocate_condition_identities`, `condition_operations.py:42-66`, guard `:56-62`; the message `Cannot add N condition(s): the new condition numbers would reach X, past 2147483647, the highest condition number.`) for Duplicate, copy to another bid and New Condition, and renumbers with temporaries that stay above the highest number when they fit, else below both the lowest number and 1; the owner's item 3 asked iOS to do the same. iOS: `ost_store::duplicate_condition` (until M1 the only allocator; `insert_condition` too since M1 (2026-11-24)) refuses before writing with the Windows text, shown in the existing "Duplicate Error" alert; the section "Closed by the Windows commit `49a2d59` and the decisions of job 6" has the code, the boundaries, the list of the other iOS paths and the 14 tests (`CondAlloc01_NewNumbers.*`, `CondAlloc01_DuplicateFailed.*`, `CondAlloc01_DuplicateLimit.*`). The sentence "Not changed (owner note g)" of the previous bullet is superseded: owner note g is resolved for `duplicate_condition`, `ost_store::shift_condition_ref_nos` stays unguarded and has no production caller. Device check DEV-11. Status stays fixed-ui-unverified (until job 8, see the end of this bullet). **Decided by the owner 2026-11-21 (job 7):** the overflow alert of Duplicate keeps the title "Duplicate Error" (Windows titles it "Duplicate Conditions"; the message text is identical to Windows); no change; two duplicates at 2147483646 are pinned in Core (`refuse_new_condition_numbers(2147483646, 2)` gives X = 2147483648, plural) and by sequential store calls, and are not reachable through the UI (Duplicate copies one condition per request). **Updated 2026-11-21 (job 8):** verified on device: DEV-05 and DEV-11 both PASS (owner, 2026-11-21, owner's iPhone, IPA of run 44953904172); status fixed (was fixed-ui-unverified).

#### Code change 4: UND-09, "Count as Negative Quantity" changes only the pressed takeoff, in one undoable entry

- **Decision**: only the pressed takeoff changes, in ONE undoable entry (like Windows `on_set_negative`, `ost_visualizer/presentation/handlers/plan_view_action_handler.py:2072-2116`, for a single selected takeoff). A condition that is merely selected in the sidebar no longer widens the change to every takeoff of the condition on the open pages (what iOS did before). **[Updated 2026-11-28 (job 11): "only the pressed takeoff" is the rule for a SINGLE takeoff; for a group (the Windows multi-selection, a pasted group) the whole group changes, see the last bullet.]**
- **Files**: `Core/Domain/NegativeChange.hpp` (new; `negative_changes_for_press` at :31, `negative_values` at :44), `Controllers/ViewController/ViewController.mm:589` (`_setNegative:forPressedTakeoff:` uses the Core decision), `Controllers/ViewController/Components/UndoRedoController.h` and `.mm:65` (undo and redo apply the old and new flags), `Config/Tests/Settings/test_negative_press.cpp` (new).
- **Tests** (6 new): `Und09_NegativePress.AConditionSelectedInTheSidebarWidensNothing` (`Config/Tests/Settings/test_negative_press.cpp:73`), `Und09_NegativePress.TheOtherTakeoffsOfTheConditionStayOutOfTheEntry` (`Config/Tests/Settings/test_negative_press.cpp:84`), `Und09_NegativePress.TheOldFlagOfThePressedTakeoffIsRecorded` (`Config/Tests/Settings/test_negative_press.cpp:100`), `Und09_NegativePress.AnUnknownUidGivesNoChange` (`Config/Tests/Settings/test_negative_press.cpp:114`), `Und09_NegativePress.AnEntryWritesTheOldFlagOnUndoAndTheNewFlagOnRedo` (`Config/Tests/Settings/test_negative_press.cpp:122`), `Und09_NegativePress.OneUndoRestoresThePressedTakeoffAndOneRedoAppliesItAgain` (`Config/Tests/Settings/test_negative_press.cpp:132`). They exercise Core only.
- **Failing first**: the Core press decision was first added with the old rule (a condition selected in the sidebar widens the change to every takeoff of the condition on the open pages); against it 5 of the 6 new tests failed on assertions (for example changes.size 3 != 1, and in the undo chain test takeoff 500 was changed too) and 1 passed (AnUnknownUidGivesNoChange, which the old rule already met): TESTS: 6 PASSED: 1 FAILED: 5. The red run was against that old rule placed in Core, not against the .mm (the host harness cannot run .mm); negative_values was moved verbatim from the .mm. **Review**: CONFIRMED-FIXED by an independent reviewer, who ran three mutations of NegativeChange.hpp (the file was restored byte-identically, checked by sha256): widening the press to the whole condition (the old behavior) failed 5 of 6 tests; recording no old flag and returning a change for an unknown uid failed 5 of 6; swapping use_old in negative_values failed 2 of 6 (undo and redo swapped). The .mm call site cannot be mutated on the host, so the reviewer read it. The Core test has no selection input: it proves one change per press with the old flag recorded, not that the .mm passes the right uid and flag.
- **Residual risk and device check**: the context-menu, undo and redo path (`_setNegative:forPressedTakeoff:`, the undo buttons) was not run on a device.
- **Updated 2026-11-28 (job 11) (owner decision 3, 2026-11-28): a pasted group.** "Count as Negative Quantity" on a pasted GROUP (owner question 5): the Windows rule replaces the pressed-takeoff-only default for a group; a single takeoff is unchanged. The Windows "group" is the CURRENT plan-view multi-selection (after Paste the pasted takeoffs, children included, plus annotations stay selected; annotation and unknown uids are dropped; no descendant expansion). Rule (`plan_view_action_handler.py:2134-2181` on_set_negative; `input_handler.py:3247-3251`; `view_context_menu.py:162-196`; `takeoff.py:136`): the item is offered only if the selection holds at least one root takeoff and NO child takeoff (hole or attachment); it is checked only when EVERY selected takeoff is negative; the new value is "not all negative" for EVERY selected takeoff (mixed or all positive -> all True, all negative -> all False), NOT taken from the pressed item; ONE undo entry records (uid, old) and (uid, new) for ALL members, also those already at the target; undo restores each takeoff's own old flag. Ported in Core: `Core/Domain/NegativeChange.hpp` `negative_toggle_for_press(takeoffs, group_uids, pressed_uid)` returning {offered, checked, changes}; the GROUP path (two or more uids that resolve to takeoffs and the pressed takeoff is a member) is the Windows rule, the SINGLE path (empty group, group of one, pressed takeoff not a member) is exactly the old behaviour (no hole gate: iOS still offers the item on a single pressed hole or attachment, Windows hides it: recorded divergence kept). iOS has no multi-selection and no paste before M2, so the Objective-C long-press handler passes an EMPTY group (identical behaviour through the new Core function); the M2 paste wiring passes the pasted uids as the group (`NEW_FEATURES_PLAN.md` N-M2-04, question resolved). UND-09 single-takeoff row stays (single takeoff only; the group is the Windows rule). Tests: suite `Und09_NegativeGroup` (9 tests) in `Config/Tests/Settings/test_negative_press.cpp`; the six `Und09_NegativePress` tests stay valid. Owner flags (defaults = exact Windows): a pasted group that contains a hole or attachment has no item; the check mark shows only when ALL are negative and a press sets all negative. Tests: `Und09_NegativeGroup.AnAllPositiveGroupBecomesAllNegative`, `Und09_NegativeGroup.AnAllPositiveGroupOfThreeIsOneEntryOfThree`, `Und09_NegativeGroup.AMixedGroupSetsEveryMemberNegativeAndTheNegativeOnesStayInTheEntry`, `Und09_NegativeGroup.AnAllNegativeGroupBecomesAllPositive`, `Und09_NegativeGroup.AHoleOrAnAttachmentInTheGroupHidesTheItem`, `Und09_NegativeGroup.APressedTakeoffOutsideTheGroupActsAlone`, `Und09_NegativeGroup.AnnotationAndUnknownUidsOfTheGroupAreSkipped`, `Und09_NegativeGroup.TheSinglePathIsTheOldBehaviour`, `Und09_NegativeGroup.OneUndoRestoresEachFlagOfTheGroupAndOneRedoAppliesThemAgain`. Device: row P4-07 checks the single takeoff only (no group row is possible before M2).

## Possible Windows defects (for the Windows side)

Found on 2026-11-19 while researching the kept items and the two deliberate deviations: possible defects of the Windows app at HEAD `6d4786f`, not changes made here. **Updated 2026-11-20 (job 5):** the Windows repository moved to `b9902e6`, which fixed most of them. Every row was re-read at `b9902e6` and starts with its outcome there; the "Windows file:line" column is `b9902e6` numbering, "What was wrong" and "Scenario" describe the Windows behaviour at `6d4786f` (history) for the rows Windows fixed and are still current for the others. The wording that called the behaviour accidental or unsafe was removed for the 12 rows Windows fixed; "possible Windows defect" stays only for the rows Windows did not fix: EXP-13 and EXP-M02 (changed differently in `b9902e6`), EXP-17, COND-FOLDER-01 and IMP-20 (2b) (unchanged apart from a log message for the last one). iOS copies none of the still-open ones (or copies them on purpose, as the decision says). Nothing was changed in the Windows repository. Windows paths are relative to its root. Each row lists the main code locations; the complete list of cited locations (further consumers and the Windows tests that pin the behaviour) is in the `possibleWindowsDefect` and `furtherPossibleWindowsDefects` fields of the item in `PARITY_DIVERGENCES.json` and in the `possible Windows defect` lines of the item block in `PARITY_MAP.md`. 18 rows: 14 from the decisions of 2026-11-19, 3 from earlier decisions (EXP-17, EXP-M02, LP-22) and 1 from a note that was still open then (COND-PLACE-01 note 2). Outcomes: fixed on Windows 12, changed differently on Windows and decided on iOS 2, no longer a defect 1, unchanged on Windows and kept 2, log message only 1 (12 + 2 + 1 + 2 + 1 = 18). "Severity" and "Confidence" are those of the 2026-11-19 research ("was" marks a fixed row).

**Updated 2026-11-27 (job 9):** re-checked at Windows `d26893c` (job 9). The 18 rows below and their text are the state at `b9902e6` (history); the table "Re-check at Windows `d26893c`" after the main table gives, per row, the outcome now, the Windows file:line at `d26893c`, what iOS does now and the pin test. Outcomes at `d26893c`: fixed on Windows 13 (the 12 of `b9902e6` stay fixed, and COND-FOLDER-01 was fixed in `0a24294`), changed differently on Windows 2 (EXP-13, EXP-M02: still possible defects), no longer a defect 1 (COND-PLACE-01 note 2), unchanged and still a possible Windows defect 2 (EXP-17, IMP-20 (2b)) (13 + 2 + 1 + 2 = 18). Four rows are therefore still possible Windows defects: EXP-13, EXP-M02, EXP-17 and IMP-20 (2b). The job 6 oddities of COND-ELEV-01 (1) to (4) and (6), of COND-M08 (1), (2) and (4) and of DLG-FINITE-01 (PWD-1, PWD-3, PWD-4) are closed by Windows `0a24294` (each bullet below says so); the section "Closed by the Windows commits `0a24294`..`d26893c` and the decisions of job 9 (2026-11-27)" holds the table of closed items and the list "Still defects on Windows at `d26893c`".

**Updated 2026-11-27 (parity pass 3):** re-checked at Windows `d1c430a`. Of the four rows that were still possible Windows defects at `d26893c` (EXP-13, EXP-M02, EXP-17, IMP-20 (2b)), EXP-13, EXP-M02 and EXP-17 are no longer defects for the winding: every generator is outward and the flip is gone from the boolean pass and the OBJ writer (`ost_visualizer/presentation/visualization/utils/mesh.py:62`, `exporters/adapters/obj_writer.py:20-25`; Windows test `tests/integration/geometry/test_outward_winding_per_shape.py`), so the sentences in the rows and the bullets below that say "one OBJ can still hold both windings" describe `6d4786f`, `b9902e6` and `d26893c` (history). Only IMP-20 (2b) stays a possible Windows defect of the 18 rows. The decisions on iOS stand (outward meshes, no byte-identical vertex order for EXP-17).

**Updated 2026-11-21 (job 6): possible Windows defects found while porting `49a2d59`** (not among the 18 above; each is ported as is, or not ported on an owner decision, and listed for the Windows side; paths relative to the Windows repository root, lines of `49a2d59`). Every statement about the elevation rule was re-run in memory on 2026-11-21 against the real `elevation_text_inches` and the verbatim regex of `edit_condition_dialog.py:72` (nothing was written into the Windows tree).

COND-ELEV-01, the closing-inch-mark rule (`ost_visualizer/presentation/dialogs/edit_condition_dialog.py:72`, the block `:1437-1461`, the refusal `:1444-1445`):

- (1) One ASCII `"` anywhere in the text switches the whole rule off (`'"' not in elev_value`, `:1444`), so the silent truncation the rule exists to prevent still happens with a stray quote: `5'x"`, `5'6x"`, `5'abc"` and `5'x1/2"` save as 60 inches, `10'6.3"` saves as 120 and `6"5'7` saves as 60. **Confirmed by the owner 2026-11-21 (job 7):** recorded as a possible Windows defect, ported as is. **[Job 9, Windows `d26893c`: CLOSED by Windows `0a24294`: a stray quote no longer switches the rule off (`edit_condition_dialog.py:1622-1651`); iOS ported it, the owner decision "ported as is" is withdrawn]**
- (2) The character class is ASCII only (`[0-9A-Za-z]`, no `IGNORECASE`): `5'` followed by e acute, `_`, Arabic-Indic three (U+0663), fullwidth six (U+FF16), U+017F, U+212A or a CJK character is accepted as 60 inches although something non-blank follows the mark. **[Job 9, Windows `d26893c`: CLOSED by Windows `0a24294`: a non-blank character of any script after a foot mark is refused (`edit_condition_dialog.py:184-186`); iOS ported it]**
- (3) U+201D and U+2033 (and U+201C, U+2019) do not count as an inch mark: `5'6` with U+2033 is refused, `5'6` with U+2033 and `"` is accepted. **[Job 9, Windows `d26893c`: CLOSED by Windows `0a24294`: U+2033 and U+201D are inch marks in the parser, the dialog and the loader (`elevation_text.py:31-39`); U+201C and U+2019 are not; iOS ported it]**
- (4) The lone-x exemption needs the x to be the last non-blank character of the whole text and is decided per foot mark: `5'x` is accepted, `5'x'` refused, `5'_'x` accepted; U+200B, U+FEFF and U+180E after the x are not white space for Python's `\s`, so `5'x` followed by one of them is refused. **[Job 9, Windows `d26893c`: CLOSED by Windows `0a24294`: a lone x may be followed by U+180E, U+200B, U+200C, U+200D, U+2060 and U+FEFF; the other parts of (4) (the x must be the last non-blank character of the whole text) are unchanged; iOS ported it]**
- (5) The `\s*` between the mark and the character is unreachable for a first foot mark (the shared parser refuses that text first) and `$` is redundant after `\s*` (harmless). **[Job 9, Windows `d26893c`: UNCHANGED (harmless)]**
- (6) Pre-existing, not new: the dialog catches only `ValueError` (`:1442`), so a feet run beyond the float range raises an uncaught `OverflowError` in the Windows slot (checked in memory: it raises); iOS refuses it with the normal message (an existing deliberate deviation, `CondElev01_Validation.AFeetRunBeyondTheFloatRangeIsRefusedWithTheNormalMessage`). **[Job 9, Windows `d26893c`: CLOSED by Windows `0a24294`: the dialog refuses a feet run beyond the float range with the normal message (`elevation_text.py:145-158`, `edit_condition_dialog.py:1622-1627`); iOS and Windows now agree, no deviation is left]**
- (7) Consequence for stored data (parity, owner-visible, note l): a stored name such as `Footing @T 5'6` opens with `5'6` in the Elevation field and EVERY dirty save of that condition is refused until the elevation is fixed or cleared. Windows validates the whole form on every dirty save (`_apply_changes` `:1760-1765`: `if not self._dirty: return True`, then `_validate_and_build_dto`, whose refusal returns None; `_on_ok` `:1823-1827` then does not close); iOS does the same (`EditConditionViewController.mm` `_applyChanges`, `if (!_dirty) return YES; if (![self _validateAndCollectTextFields]) return NO`, `:359-363`). Read from the code on both sides, not run on iOS when this was written (the host tests pin the rule and the loader, not this combination). **Observed on device 2026-11-21 (job 8):** DEV-10 PASS (owner's iPhone, IPA of run 44953904172): such a name opens with `5'6` in the field and any other edit of it is refused until the elevation is fixed or cleared. **[Job 9, Windows `d26893c`: UNCHANGED, parity (`edit_condition_dialog.py:2030-2036`, `:1622-1651`; iOS pin for the loader half: `CondElev02_ClosingInchMark.TheLoaderStillReadsAStoredFeetDigitNameAsFeet`, no host pin for the combination)]**

COND-M08, the "Unknown (N)" style item (`edit_condition_dialog.py`):

- (1) NOT ported, decided by the owner 2026-11-21 (job 7) (note k, closed): Windows resets a stored quantity that the type's option list does not offer to "(none)" on EVERY save of that condition (`:897-898`, `:984-990`, `:1658-1664`). For an unknown type the list is always the Count list, so a type-7 condition with a stored quantity (for example calc 11 / uom 5) saves `{'calc_type1': 0, 'uom1': -1, 'notes': ...}` on Windows, while iOS writes only the notes; the owner's rule and the Windows test use quantity 0. A possible Windows defect, silent data loss (the owner's classification of 2026-11-21, job 7; job 6 called it a "wrong-number risk"): the stored quantity is lost without any message, and it also hits unknown types, because for an unknown type the option list is always the Count list; confidence high (run headless against the real Windows dialog while job 6 ran); job 6 had left the question whether to port it open ("asks nothing unless the owner wants the Windows reset ported"), the owner decided: not ported. The same happens on Windows for a known Count with calc 11 and not for an Area. **[Job 9, Windows `d26893c`: CLOSED by Windows `0a24294`: Windows keeps a stored quantity that the type's list does not offer (`edit_condition_dialog.py:1913-1925`); the "possible Windows defect, silent data loss" was real and is fixed; iOS is parity, pins added (`CondM08_UnknownStyle.AStoredQuantityTheTypeDoesNotOfferSurvivesANotesOnlySave` and others); new possible Windows defect: such a quantity cannot be cleared with "(none)" any more (section "New possible Windows defects found in job 9")]**
- (2) Windows keeps the disabled "Unknown (N)" item after a successful Save or after the user picked a real type: `_sync_unknown_style_item` runs only in `_load_condition` (`:844`). Ported as is (the iOS instance variable also lives until the next load). **[Job 9, Windows `d26893c`: CLOSED by Windows `0a24294`: `_after_successful_save` calls `_sync_unknown_style_item` (`edit_condition_dialog.py:1073-1081`, `:1126-1137`), so the entry is gone after a real style is saved; iOS ported it in job 9 (commit `cb979c9`; "Ported as is" above is superseded; fixed-ui-unverified)]**
- (3) Picking a real style from "Unknown (N)" carries nothing over and writes the Spacing although unchanged (`:1640` `if type_changed or spacing != cond.spacing`): the generic type-change rule, ported through `apply_style_change` and `condition_changes_for_save`. **[Job 9, Windows `d26893c`: UNCHANGED, parity (`edit_condition_dialog.py:1786`, `:1838`, `:1895`; pin `CondM08_UnknownStyle.ChoosingARealTypeWritesTheTypeAndItsDefaults`)]**
- (4) Windows writes `abs(rise)` and `abs(run)` on a notes-only edit of an Area-like condition with a negative stored rise or run (`:1608`, `:1620`): the generic Area rule DLG-RISE-01, now also reached by the unknown type; the Objective-C half on iOS (the fields are parsed with `absolute:YES`) cannot be host-tested. **[Job 9, Windows `d26893c`: CLOSED by Windows `0a24294` (DLG-RISE-01): a notes-only save keeps a negative stored rise / run (`edit_condition_dialog.py:1860`, `:1882`; the Linear branch `:1808`, `:1830`); iOS ported it in job 9 (commit `9327212`; pins `CondM08_UnknownStyle.ANotesOnlyEditKeepsANegativeRiseAndRunOfAnAreaLikeCondition` and two more); the statement that the Objective-C half "cannot be host-tested" stays true]**
- (5) Windows-only: tower-crane note warnings on a type change or a notes edit (`:1665-1686`): not ported (no iOS feature). **[Job 9, Windows `d26893c`: UNCHANGED (`edit_condition_dialog.py:1925-1960`), no iOS counterpart]**

DLG-FINITE-01, the allocator (`ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/components/condition_operations.py`):

- PWD-1 (low): `renumber_conditions` raises `ConditionNumberOverflowError` (`:780-784`) but its `except Exception` (`:802-808`) has no dedicated re-raise for it (the three allocating callers have `except ConditionNumberOverflowError: raise`, `:141-142`, `:241-242`, `:344-345`) and re-raises only when `_record_caught_mutation_error` is true (on the Access path a pyodbc.Error inside a transaction only, `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/mdb_writer.py:216-219`; the SQL-Server writer returns true in every case, `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/sql/writer.py:1199-1203`, and so re-raises: that path was not checked further), so on the Access path the error is logged and False is returned; the handler then shows the generic "Failed to renumber conditions" (`condition_action_handler.py:1357-1365`) and the new renumber text is never shown. No iOS counterpart. **[Job 9, Windows `d26893c`: CLOSED by Windows `0a24294`: `renumber_conditions` re-raises `ConditionNumberOverflowError` and the service returns its text as the user message (`condition_operations.py:754-815`, raise `:792`, re-raise `:812-813`; `project_write_service.py:1478-1535`); no iOS counterpart]**
- PWD-2 (info): only the local Access path carries `user_message`; the SQL Server queue paths (`queue_conditions_duplicate`, `condition_action_handler.py:914`, `:1063`) were not changed and were not checked. No iOS counterpart. **[Job 9, Windows `d26893c`: UNCHANGED (`ost_visualizer/presentation/handlers/condition_action_handler.py:991-1000`), no iOS counterpart]**
- PWD-3 (info): the docstring of `ConditionNumberOverflowError` (`ost_visualizer/application/dtos/update_condition_dto.py:10`) still says "A renumber would shift a condition number past MAX_CONDITION_NUMBER" although it is now also raised for new conditions and for the temporaries. **[Job 9, Windows `d26893c`: CLOSED by Windows `0a24294` (`update_condition_dto.py:9-11` names new, duplicated, renumbered and temporary numbers)]**
- PWD-4 (info, existing): a plain failed Duplicate without a message shows nothing on Windows (`test_a_failed_plain_duplicate_does_not_warn`); iOS keeps its "Duplicate Error" alert (DBP-11 wording), unchanged. Decided by the owner 2026-11-21 (job 7): the title stays "Duplicate Error"; the message text of the overflow alert is identical to Windows. **[Job 9, Windows `d26893c`: CLOSED by Windows `0a24294`: a failed plain Duplicate now shows "Failed to duplicate the condition." plus the locked hint, silent only when the bid is locked (`condition_action_handler.py:1045`, `:1293-1307`; `texts.py:745-750`); iOS has had that alert since job 6, the title "Duplicate Error" stays (decision of job 7)]**

Suggested direction of each fix (from the 2026-11-19 research, not applied here). **Updated 2026-11-20 (job 5):** Windows applied most of them in `b9902e6`; each bullet says what it did. The bullets of the rows that are still possible Windows defects stay suggestions: EXP-13 and EXP-M02, EXP-17, COND-FOLDER-01, IMP-20 (2b). Check against the Windows tests first: **Updated 2026-11-27 (job 9):** of the bullets that were still suggestions only EXP-13 / EXP-M02, EXP-17 and IMP-20 (2b) are still open on Windows; COND-FOLDER-01 was done.

- DBP-05: done in `b9902e6` (the first option: `1.0 if bid_row.TakeoffIncrements is None else float(...)`, `hierarchy_reader.py:126-130`).
- EXP-12: done in `b9902e6`: every character below 0x20 and U+007F becomes `_` (`base_exporter.py:15`, `:200`; the Unicode line separators U+0085, U+2028 and U+2029 were not added); tests `test_base_exporter.py:123-138`.
- EXP-13 and EXP-M02: done differently in `b9902e6`, not with the `boolean_applied` flag (which the native code still sets, `module_geometry.cpp:90`): a positive whose bounding box does not strictly overlap a same-type negative bounding box is returned as the original mesh and only the positives that may be cut are flipped (`boolean_operations.py:37-111`). The remaining suggestion: normalise the winding once after the pass, or make the generators consistent (see EXP-17).
- EXP-16: done in `b9902e6`: CR and LF become a space in the comment lines (`obj_exporter.py:9-10`, `:50`, `:74`); other control characters are not replaced there (the EXP-12 sanitiser handles them in names).
- IMP-20 (1): done in `b9902e6`: `OverflowError` is caught at `import_operations.py:608` and the characterisation test was replaced (`test_import_operations.py:796`, `:3099`).
- IMP-20 (2b): still a suggestion: validate the text against latin-1 in the annotation editor with a specific message, or, if the legacy reader really is Windows-1252, encode with cp1252 and keep the error for the rest (`b9902e6` only added a log warning).
- IMP-12: done in `b9902e6`: `_files_have_same_content` starts with `if not second.is_file(): return False` (`osp_importer.py:428-430`).
- COND-UPD-02: done in `b9902e6` with an `is not None` guard instead of `int(current_row[0] or 0)`: a NULL RefNo is different from the new number and is never shifted (`condition_operations.py:612-613`).
- COND-FOLDER-01: still a suggestion (not done in `b9902e6`): build the summary row type name with the same rule as `_group_label`: `cdn_type_name if cdn_type_uid else SUMMARY_UNASSIGNED_LABEL`. **[Job 9: done by Windows `0a24294` in another way: the summary row uses `(cdn_type_name if cdn_type_uid else "") or SUMMARY_UNASSIGNED_LABEL`, `condition_summary_service.py:446-448`.]**
- COND-STYLE-02 (metric): done in `b9902e6`: `_type_default_thickness` converts `thickness_metric` with `mm_to_inches` in a metric bid (`edit_condition_dialog.py:1236-1239`).
- COND-STYLE-02 (spacing): done in `b9902e6`: on a type change the thickness and spacing fields are persisted (`type_changed or ...`, `edit_condition_dialog.py:1559`, `:1589`, `:1624`).
- COND-ELEV-01: done in `b9902e6`: the dialog validates with `elevation_text_inches` and the placeholder is `e.g. 3.2 m` (it refuses a bare metric number instead of converting it to `<n> cm`, as the research suggested).
- LP-24: done in `b9902e6` (the second option: shared template rows are dropped from the bulk write and from the captured uids).
- RPV-27 (b): done differently in `b9902e6`: the marker is a child of an anchor item (`takeoff_renderer.py:385-416`), so the page transform moves it with the shape, instead of mapping the centroid through the transform.
- EXP-17: still a suggestion: make the generator tables consistent (reverse `get_triangle_mesh_faces` and `get_rhombus_mesh_faces` to wind like the box and radial tables) or make `_transform_faces` use its `mesh_type`; add a signed-volume test per shape (the Windows integration test of `b9902e6` only pins the current split).
- LP-22 and COND-PLACE-01 note 2: done in `b9902e6` (the first option: `update_all_layer_visibility` leaves the conditions without an own layer visible, `project_data_service.py:675-700`).

Kept, researched, judged not a defect: DBP-04 (the NULL half), BRW-09, DBP-33, EXP-21, EXP-09, IMP-20 (2) and (3) on the import side, COND-FOLDER-01 (the loader sentinel itself), COND-ELEV-01 (memory versus reload), RPV-29, RPV-26, RPV-27 (a), RPV-16, UND-02, GEO-M02, DLG-FINITE-01 (Windows rejects, iOS now copies it), UND-09 (iOS now copies Windows); from earlier decisions EXP-15, EXP-18, IMP-17, DBP-34, DBP-M05, COND-EXTRA-01, GEO-15, MSH-21, RPV-22, CAM-02; and COND-PLACE-01 note 1 (an edge case only, and it was an iOS divergence rather than a Windows defect: Windows has had the Image-layer guard all along, iOS ported it in job 5). **Updated 2026-11-27 (job 9):** EXP-21 is no longer "judged not a defect / kept without a question": Windows changed the wording of its export and import alerts (see the EXP-21 row and "Decisions for the owner after parity pass 2"); COND-FOLDER-01 (the loader sentinel itself) is fixed on Windows (re-check table). **Updated 2026-11-28 (job 11):** EXP-21 is decided by the owner (2026-11-28): the Windows wording is adopted, the keep decision is superseded; UND-09: a pasted group follows the Windows rule (single takeoff unchanged) (section "Closed by the owner decisions of job 11 (2026-11-28)").

## Decisions for the owner after parity pass 2 (job 9, 2026-11-27)

Nothing here blocks anything. Each question has a default that stays in force until the owner decides; nothing below was changed in job 9 unless it says so. Windows paths are `d26893c` line numbers.

|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **RESOLVED 2026-11-28 (job 11): the owner decided to ADOPT the current Windows wording; the text below is the state of pass 2.** **EXP-21, export and import alert wording.** The owner decision of 2026-11-19 was "keep iOS" (the export alert says "Error creating OBJ export: <reason>", the import alert shows the diagnostic under "Import Failed"). Its basis changed: Windows `c46d751` / `d26893c` removed "Export function returned False." and the "Error creating ... export:" prefix and now shows user sentences ("The file could not be written. Please ensure you have write permissions to the destination folder and try again."; "No file was written. | 2 | **RESOLVED 2026-11-27 (parity pass 3): ported; iOS now shows the Windows "Undo" / "Redo" alerts (the owner decided "port").** **Undo / redo failure alert** (A3-31, A7-14). (2) COND-PLACE-01 note 1: ported (the Image-layer exemption on the single toggle, plus the tap guard that Windows has). (3) COND-PLACE-01 note 2: not a defect any more after the Windows LP-22 fix; iOS matches, pinned. The rows below are the 2026-11-19 questions with the answer added; "What iOS did meanwhile" is the behaviour of 2026-11-19.

|---|---|---|---|

(`Core/Domain/ConditionFormRules.cpp`, `parse_condition_number`, value > `INT_MAX`; `Condition::ref_no` is an int). ### For information, no decision needed (10)

No Windows file:line is recorded in these notes except the one in the DBP-11 note; the item of the same id in `PARITY_MAP.md` has the Windows citations.

- IMP-14: an unreadable or non-cabinet package says "Invalid OSP file format" where Windows says "found 0" (iOS wording kept).
- DBP-11: the iOS failure dialogs keep the Windows hint "The database may be locked by another application." [Updated 2026-11-28 (job 11): not the four generic layer failures any more (owner decision 2: the Windows layers dialog has no hint); the browser and folder failures keep it, and "Delete Error" carries only the Windows sentence "Failed to delete projects. The database may be locked by another application."]; "New Project / Failed to create project.", "Save Failed / Failed to save the display settings.", "Duplicate Error", "Delete Error" and the "Not Saved" alert for failed forward writes are iOS wording where Windows only logs; the "Layer Visibility" alert uses the title and text of the Windows Layers dialog (`ost_visualizer/presentation/dialogs/layers_dialog.py:584`, `:605`), which Windows shows only there, not for its sidebar toggles. **Updated 2026-11-21 (job 7):** the owner decided that the overflow alert of Duplicate keeps the title "Duplicate Error" (Windows: "Duplicate Conditions"; the message text is identical); this note stays information only.
- Geometry tolerance (GEO-M02, geometry): Qt's clipper tolerance band cannot be reproduced; iOS uses one tolerance (1e-12 of the candidate size plus 8 ulp); identical to Windows for exactly touching geometry and beyond about 1e-7 relative. This is not the decided GEO-M02 point (validity in OST coordinates); the decision does not cover it.
- CAB (import-pass1): iOS is deliberately stricter than the Windows cabinet library on inconsistent cabinet headers.
- Quantities and formatting: Windows `math.ceil` raising on a non-finite quotient is not reproduced (the quantity path never throws); feet digit runs of 309 or more digits (Windows `OverflowError`) return `(0.0, false)` on iOS; Windows quirks ported as they are: `format_quantity_number` prints `-0` / `-0.00`, `display_to_mm` strips any run of trailing `m`.
- Export: a cut positive whose faces were all removed by `remove_degenerate_faces` is not counted as surviving (Windows would export an empty file); the Windows ERROR log of a failed `boolean_union` is not ported (only the WARNING).
- Plan view: a stored negative or non-finite `BidPages` Width/Height is treated as missing (612 x 792) where Windows carries it into the transform; a rotation that is not a multiple of 90 draws an unrotated canvas and no takeoffs; image layers named "image" are decided by smallest sequence, then uid.
- Plan view (RPV-32): Windows draws small mid handles on the in-progress area polyline preview, iOS only on the rectangle being dragged.
- LP-12: writing the saved page back on pick is not ported (optional per item); `load_project` fails a bid with two `BidSettings` rows like Windows.
- Undo: undo of a delete selects only the deleted takeoff (iOS has one selected takeoff); an older hazard: a takeoff selected in 3D stays selected after switching to 2D although its page may not be the 2D page.

### iOS-internal leftovers (7, no behaviour impact)

From the final review of the Objective-C wave:

- Four near-identical "present an OK alert from the top-most presented controller" helpers in `ViewController` categories written by different steps: `_presentConditionAlertWithTitle:message:` (`ViewController+Conditions.mm`), `_presentSaveFailure` (`ViewController.mm`), `_presentLayerVisibilityFailure` (`ViewController+Layers.mm`), `_presentExportAlertWithTitle:message:` (`ViewController+Export.mm`, which presents on `self` without walking `presentedViewController`). Cosmetic.
- `isCountLike()` in `PlanView/PlanView.mm:27` repeats a count-or-attachment test that `Core/Geometry/takeoff_footprint.cpp` already makes. Harmless.
- No caller except tests: `ost_store::shift_condition_ref_nos` (the update now shifts atomically), the one-argument `ImportService +loadProjectWithUID:`, `ost_meshing::generate_takeoff_meshes` (a thin wrapper), `RenderConfig::is_ost()` (deprecated alias, two tests). **Updated 2026-11-21 (job 6):** `shift_condition_ref_nos` (`RefNo = RefNo + 1`) also has no overflow guard; it was left unchanged on purpose because it creates no new number (owner note g is resolved for `duplicate_condition`).
- Double work on open (performance only): `DatabaseViewController` preflights the bid with `loadProjectWithUID:project:error:` and `ViewController` loads it again.
- Hole grouping runs over all takeoffs of the page (like the old hole map), not only the visible ones.
- The edit pan hit-tests at the recognition location (after the pan threshold), not at touch-down (RPV-M07 only fixes the start point); `clearTapSelection` also clears `_draggingUID`, so a page-bar tap by a second finger during a drag would drop `didEndDrag`.
- Tooling outside the repository: the self-test of the Objective-C syntax checker fails in its case "a" because the anchor text it looks for was in a file that was rewritten (5 of 6 cases pass); neither the checker nor the code is affected.

Closed meanwhile: the COND-PLACE-01 hand-off between the conditions and layers steps (reload the Conditions list after a layer toggle, suspend and restore placement) was executed afterwards: Core `suspended_placement_for_layer_hide` and `restore_of_suspended_placement`, wired in `ViewController+Layers.mm` (fixed-ui-unverified); what remained of that item were the two notes above. **Updated 2026-11-20 (job 5):** both are closed too (note 1 ported, note 2 not a defect), so nothing of COND-PLACE-01 is open.

### B-004: PDF annotation export
- **Status**: Not implemented (outside this job: no new features)
- **Current state**: no PDF writer on iOS. Search found no PDFKit, UIGraphicsPDFRenderer, CGPDFContext or qpdf; the only PDF code is CGPDFDocument reading in `PlanView/Components/PDFCanvasView.mm`. `ExportService` exposes OBJ, DXF and Three.js only. iOS has no annotation model (annotation rows are written only by the generic .ost import and deleted only on permanent bid delete and together with the takeoff or condition that links them).
- **Impact**: blocks exporting takeoffs and annotations as PDF markup. Does not block viewing, 3D rendering, OBJ/DXF/Three.js export or import.
- **Windows evidence**: `cpp_extensions/src/pdf/pdf_writer.cpp`, `cpp_extensions/src/pdf/bluebeam_annotation.cpp`, `ost_visualizer/presentation/visualization/exporters/pdf_exporter.py`, `cpp_extensions/CMakeLists.txt` (QPDF).
- **iOS evidence**: `Services/ExportService.h`, `Core/Export/` (OBJ, DXF, Three.js writers only), `PlanView/Components/PDFCanvasView.mm`.

### B-009: OST/OSP export (round trip)
- **Status**: Not implemented (outside this job)
- **Current state**: no .ost or .osp writer and no CAB writer (no deflate or compress call in `Core/`; `CabExtractor.cpp` only inflates). The import now keeps the Windows data (BidEmployees, PayClasses and AccessLevels are imported, IMP-11; values are converted by column type, IMP-05), but a writer would still need the Windows byte formats that were deliberately kept out (IMP-20 (2) and (3) and DBP-34, decided to stay iOS) and the Access column types (the iOS schema uses INTEGER, TEXT, REAL and BLOB).
- **Impact**: iOS can import .ost and .osp but cannot write an edited project back to a desktop-compatible package.
- **Windows evidence**: `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/exporters/ost_exporter.py`, `ost_visualizer/presentation/visualization/exporters/osp_exporter.py`.
- **iOS evidence**: `Services/ExportService.h`, `Core/Import/CabExtractor.cpp` (read only).

In run 44654559214 the unbounded build started about 60 and then about 110 compiler processes, each wave within the same second, on a runner that GitHub documents as 3 cores and about 7 GB RAM for this standard size (documented, not measured: the job log does not print it). Neither fix changed app code: `5dc7b2b` changed tests and docs only, `787a57d` changed only the workflow (the host-test build is bounded) and `BUILD.md`.

### Not reproducible on iOS
- **EXP-24** (cannot-reproduce): the DXF LINE entity order is the iteration order of a `std::unordered_set` (`Core/Geometry/feature_edges.cpp:124`, `Services/ExportService.mm:151`); it differs between STL implementations (Windows MSVC and libc++) and cannot be matched. The DXF content is the same, only the order of the lines differs.
- **Qt-specific results** (decided items above): the Qt path clipper tolerance band and point order (GEO-M02, RPV-27 (a)) and the scene-pixel evaluation (GEO-M02) have no iOS counterpart.
- **Marker anchor tie on a mirror-symmetric curved band** (RPV-27, the negative marker anchor; not reproducible bit for bit across platforms; found by the first GitHub run of the 7-commit push, 2026-11-20, run 44642251350): when the centroid of a curved linear takeoff lies outside the band, Windows `_nearest_interior_anchor` takes `min(candidates, key=squared distance)`, the first of the nearest grid centres. On a band that is mirror symmetric the two best centres are mirror images; in the doubles their squared distances come out bit-identical on the x86-64 builds here, so the first (left) one wins, as on Windows, and a last-bit change in the arc points breaks the tie. The AppleClang arm64 run gave the mirror one, 4.85 points (one grid cell) on the other side of the axis (72.5756 against 77.4244). Cause: inferred, not observed on arm64. The only calls in that path whose result can differ between platforms are `atan2`, `cos` and `sin` (Windows CRT against Apple libm); no fused multiply-add is emitted for the two files involved (checked on an aarch64-macos build with zig clang 22), and nudging one of the 53 libm results of the test case by one ulp flips the answer in 23 of 106 cases, to the value CI reported (20 of the 23 bit for bit, the rest within 2 ulp). Making the result equal to the MSVC CRT's was not attempted: it would mean in-tree `atan2`, `sin` and `cos`, which changes numerics everywhere (the owner's decision). No rule differs: an exact tie keeps the first candidate on both sides (Python `min` and the strict `<` of the C++), which `MarkerAnchor.AnExactTieKeepsTheFirstCandidateOfTheWindowsOrder` pins with dyadic inputs and no libm call. `MarkerAnchor.CountTriangleAndCurvedBand` accepts the Windows point and its mirror image. No app code changed. On a device the marker of an exactly symmetric curved band can therefore sit one grid cell to the other side of its axis than on Windows; the owner may want to look at it.

## M1 (2026-11-24): what only a device, CI or the owner can settle

Milestone M1 (branch `ios-feature-m1`, began at `06d8d69`; `NEW_FEATURES_PLAN.md`): New Condition, condition folders, layers, Set Scale, rename page, delete page, their foundations and the page menu. What is verified: the Core rules and the store functions (2907 tests, 2916 with Manifold, MSVC and clang with fused multiply-add; the host runs were made on `17b3a1a`, the code of the branch was last changed at `87cdf8c`, which changed only an Objective-C file), mutation checks, an allocation-failure probe of the layer store functions (a scratch copy, not committed), independent reviews (five whole-milestone reviewers: no blocker; the independent recheck of the fixes accepted them with no blocker or major), a syntax check of the Objective-C(++) against hand-written stubs (42 of 42 platform files, 0 errors, the 4 baseline stub warnings, exactly the baseline 6 undeclared selectors; both re-run on `87cdf8c`), and, since the GitHub build, the compile of the Objective-C(++) against the real iPhoneOS 18.5 SDK (run 45528720676 on `2eae9ed`: no error, no new first-party warning) and the check of its IPA (all checks passed). What is NOT verified, and cannot be here (Windows machine; no Xcode, simulator or device): anything at run time on a device (see the bullets below); the build is no longer on that list.

- **The build against the real SDK (done).** GitHub run 45528720676 (success on the first attempt, head `2eae9ed`) compiled the Objective-C(++) of M1 against the real iPhoneOS 18.5 SDK with no error and no new first-party warning (9 distinct, identical to the baseline of 6 `-Wundeclared-selector`, 1 `-Wprotocol` and 2 category-implements warnings, compared by file and message; `check_objc` does not report undeclared selectors, and the baseline 6 are the six that the selector scan finds), and its IPA check passed (ALL CHECKS PASSED). The last CI-verified code state of the branch is the head `2eae9ed` of that run; that of `main` stays run 45039125909. What stays open is everything on a device: install, launch, ARC and runtime behaviour, UIKit behaviour, the 20 M1 checks and the 23 carried checks.
- **The stubs of `check_objc` against the real Apple signatures (settled for compile-time facts).** They were extended for M1 (`UIControl addAction:forControlEvents:`, `UIContextualAction`, `UISwipeActionsConfiguration`, `UIDeferredMenuElement`, the `UITextInput` selection members, `UIAdaptivePresentationControllerDelegate`, `UITableView` editing); the CI compile of run 45528720676 is the comparison for compile-time facts (selectors, types, availability): it had no error. What a compile does not show, ARC ownership and behaviour at run time, is unverified until a device run.
- **Windows acceptance of the M1 rows (OI-M1-1).** The iOS database is SQLite, so the rows M1 writes cannot be checked by the Windows app before the M3 round trip; the Windows app has not opened the DeviceTestPack files. It is not a check of the pack and does not block the merge.
- **The device checks M1-01 to M1-20** (`DEVICE_CHECKS.md`, table in `PORTING_STATUS.md` under "On device"; no result yet). The rows marked iPad only (M1-01, M1-13, M1-19) are recorded "not run: no device" if no iPad is available, which the merge rule accepts only with the owner's written word.
- **Windows re-check of the M1 register and the checks of parity pass 2 (2026-11-27, job 9).** The 112 rows of the M1 register and its further items were re-read at Windows `d26893c` (table under "Possible Windows defects found while building M1"): 27 are fixed on Windows (all but one in `0a24294`, the other, A9-X4, in `5f94de1`), 4 changed differently, 81 unchanged; iOS had to change in 3 rows (NC-1, SS-2, SS-5, all ported in job 9) and keeps its own behaviour in the rest. What only a device can settle of job 9 is in `DEVICE_CHECKS.md` rows P2-01 to P2-11 (the Hide All alert, the Unknown (N) removal, the Advanced switch labels, the New Folder prefill and the folder alerts, the Set Scale refusal alert and labels, the Rename / Delete Page texts); they were not run. Nothing of M1 or of job 9 has run on a device. **Updated 2026-11-27 (parity pass 3):** the rows to test now are 59: 20 M1 (M1-01 to M1-20) + 23 carried + 11 P2 (P2-01 to P2-11) + 5 P3 (P3-01 to P3-05: the single toggle of a shared locked template layer, the delete condition alert text, the "Unknown (N)" quantity entry, the undo / redo smoke test, an undo in a locked bid); no device check exists for the metric width, the winding pins or the last-page pin (no visible effect). Re-read the M1-02 steps: a tap on a shared row now shows the alert and the pass-2 / M1 text "Failed to update layer visibility." is gone.

Device-only open points per feature (the same list is in the `deviceOnlyOpenPoints` of each record of `PARITY_DIVERGENCES.json`, `m1Items`; each record is fixed-ui-unverified):

- **M1-FND** (M1 foundations: bid lock guard, UID limit, text limits, popover anchors of the Edit Condition pickers; checks M1-01, M1-07, M1-18)
  - The popover anchors (M1-01) and the lock refusals (M1-07) were never seen on a device (iPad only for M1-01).
  - The lock bridge reads JobStatuses, which iOS never used for locking: a JobStatuses cell whose text is not valid UTF-8 empties the whole status list and every bid with a status reads as locked ('This bid is locked.' blames the bid); diagnosability only, fail-closed is the plan (review of the data path, minor 4).
- **M1-NEWCOND** (New Condition: create a condition from the Conditions list (form, writer, bridge); checks M1-02, M1-03, M1-04, M1-05, M1-06, M1-07, M1-18, M1-19, M1-20)
  - The create form, defaults per type, 'Next number', the double-tap guard (Save disabled while the write runs), the row selected and revealed without a placement, and the long-press entries were never run on a device (M1-02 to M1-06, M1-19, M1-20).
  - Whether UIKit honours modalInPresentation set at runtime on a form sheet inside a UINavigationController and fires presentationControllerDidAttemptToDismiss for the navigation controller's presentation controller with the root form as delegate (M1-06 swipe-down of an edited and of an untouched form; iPad has no interactive dismissal for a form sheet).
  - Rise, Run and Display Size % use the decimal pad, which has no minus key and offers only the locale separator: a negative Linear rise or run cannot be typed, and in a comma locale '.' cannot be typed while Core refuses ','. Pre-existing in the Edit dialog, inherited by the form; Set Scale avoided it on purpose (review of the conditions stream, minor 3).
  - A folder that holds only sub-folders has no section header, so 'New Condition in <folder>' is not offered for it (use the sub-folder, the top level or Move to Folder); judge whether an item in the folder manager is wanted (review fix report, D6).
  - Create at highest number 2147483647 shows the Windows overflow text under 'Save Failed' and the form stays open (fixture M1-07 and M1-07b of the DeviceTestPack).
- **M1-FOLDERS** (Condition folders: create, rename, delete, Move to Folder, folder manager; checks M1-04, M1-07, M1-08, M1-09, M1-10, M1-18, M1-19)
  - The folder manager (swipe left Delete / Rename, swipe right New Folder Inside, long-press menu, Edit = multi-select Delete), the header long press including an EMPTY folder, the picker check mark and '(No folder)' were never run on a device (M1-08 to M1-10).
  - Context-menu alerts are deferred with dispatch_async: whether UIKit still dropped one ('Attempt to present ... while a presentation is in progress') is only visible on a device (M1-08, M1-09).
  - After Move to Folder the highlighted row must still be the selected one (the fix keeps only selectedConditionUID); device check M1-10.
  - The folder name alerts (New Folder, New Folder Inside, Rename Folder) select their text with selectedTextRange since `87cdf8c` (they used selectAll: until then, found by the independent recheck of the fixes): confirm on the device that no Cut / Copy / Paste bar floats over the alert (M1-08).
  - After a committed write and a failed re-read the in-memory lists stay old and every writer stays enabled (the alert tells the user to reopen the bid); rare, not verifiable here (review of the data path, minor 5). **Updated 2026-11-27 (job 9):** the alert now names the action done: "The folder was created|renamed|deleted" ("The folders were deleted", "The condition was moved") ", but the conditions list could not be refreshed. Reopen the database to see the latest conditions." (title "Refresh Error"; wired in `ViewController+Conditions.mm`, commit `9b47fb1`; fixed-ui-unverified).
- **M1-LAYERS** (Layers: create, rename, delete, reorder (store writers, Layers sheet editing); checks M1-01, M1-07, M1-11, M1-12, M1-13, M1-18)
  - The Layers sheet (alert with a text field, a preferred action that starts disabled, Return reaching EditingDidEndOnExit, selectedTextRange on the next turn instead of selectAll:, swipe and context-menu actions beside the tap toggle, an alert presented from a swipe or menu handler) was never run on a device (M1-11 to M1-13).
  - The Rename Layer selection uses selectedTextRange after the review fix; the first implementation used selectAll:, which can bring up the Cut / Copy / Paste bar over the alert: confirm on the device that no edit menu appears (M1-11, rename step). Updated 2026-11-24 (job 8, M1): after `87cdf8c` no alert of the app uses selectAll: any more (the Rename Page, Rename Layer and the three folder name alerts all set selectedTextRange; grep of Controllers and Services), so the nuisance is not confined to this alert: the same confirmation covers the folder alerts (M1-08).
  - Deleting a hidden layer named Image (or the Image layer itself) changes whether the PDF is drawn: the redraw after a delete is device-only.
  - New Layer loses the typed text when a rule refuses the name (see LY-UI-6).
  - After a committed write and a failed re-read the Layers sheet keeps stale sequences that plan_layer_move uses to name the swap neighbour (rare; the user was told to reopen). **Updated 2026-11-27 (job 9):** the rename and move alerts now say "The layer was renamed|moved, but the layer list could not be refreshed. Reopen the database to see the change." (title "Refresh Error"); the delete refresh text is unchanged ("Layers were deleted, but the refresh failed." is the Windows dialog text). **[Updated 2026-11-28 (job 11): the rename and move titles are now "Rename Layer" / "Move Layer"; the generic layer write failures no longer carry the database-locked hint]**
- **M1-RENAME-PAGE** (Rename Page: rule, one-statement writer, alert from the page menu; checks M1-07, M1-14, M1-18)
  - Whether UIButton.menu with showsMenuAsPrimaryAction = NO opens on a long press while the tap still selects the page, and whether the whole-view 0.5 s long-press recogniser (no delegate) or the system context-menu recogniser wins on a page button: the guard in _handle3DLongPress: only makes the action return and cannot stop the recogniser from competing. A ready fallback exists (recogniser delegate that refuses touches inside the page bars); device check M1-14 decides.
  - After a page tap _highlightSelection dims the 2D / 3D mode chips (pre-existing: it loops over all UIButtons of both bars and nothing re-runs _highlightModeLabels); M1 repaired the chips only in the rename, delete and Set Scale paths, so a tester sees the right colour right after a rename and a dim chip after the next page tap (M1-14, M1-15 wording).
  - Scroll-offset preservation after the bar rebuild, a very long name (no truncation, N-M1-27), and the placement-flag lifecycle while an area placement is running are device-only.
  - OstDatabase::open creates an empty file for a path that does not exist (a refused call on a missing path leaves an empty database file): not reachable in the app, informational (rename report, item 7).
- **M1-DELETE-PAGE** (Delete Page: cascade writer, content scan, confirmation, reload; checks M1-07, M1-14, M1-15, M1-18)
  - The confirmation (Cancel as preferred action), the reload that selects the next page, the cleared undo history, the chips colour and the camera fit were never run on a device (M1-15).
  - After a delete the page that followed the DELETED page is selected even when another page was being viewed (DP-10 wording, deliberate): judge it on the device.
- **M1-SET-SCALE** (Set Scale: all-or-nothing page scale writer, memory twin, form, undo capture of the page scale; checks M1-07, M1-14, M1-16, M1-17, M1-18)
  - Everything about the form was never run on a device: UIButton.menu with showsMenuAsPrimaryAction, UIAction.state marks, a 17-row pull-down, an alert from an action handler, the inset-grouped layout, the keyboard room (300 pt) on iPhone SE / landscape / iPad, and the decimal-comma typing with the numbers-and-punctuation keyboard (N-M1-22).
  - The two optional UndoRedoDelegate methods reaching the controller at runtime (respondsToSelector on category methods), the capture timing in the Delete path and the replay with the real UndoHistory are device-only (M1-17); host tests cover the arithmetic only.
  - The capture is O(n x m) (page_scale_of_takeoff scans the whole takeoff vector per takeoff): about 1e8 string compares for 2000 descendants in a 50000-takeoff project, a visible hitch of a few tenths of a second; irrelevant for ordinary use, judge it with the largest bid (M1-17).
  - The save is synchronous on the main thread (also with 'Apply to all pages' on a big bid); double-tap safe; judge the duration in M1-17.
  - If the memory twin ever refuses after the database was written, _reloadProjectAfterPageScaleMismatch returns silently when the re-read fails and the Save still answers 'saved' (memory keeps old-unit positions); not reachable by design, unverifiable here (two reviews, minor).
  - A takeoff dragged or placed in the same session holds the exact double while the database holds the three-decimal text, so the twin differs from a reload by up to 0.0005 x factor until the bid is reopened (the same drift already exists after any drag).
  - A page whose stored scale is NULL or 0 and the page menu while the Conditions or Layers sheet is open at its medium detent are not exercised by the DeviceTestPack.
- **M1-PAGE-MENU** (Page action menu on the page buttons: Set Scale..., Rename Page..., Delete Page; checks M1-07, M1-14, M1-15, M1-16)
  - The whole-view long-press recogniser (no delegate) may win against the page button's context-menu interaction in 3D mode, so the menu would not open on a 3D-bar or 2D-bar page button and M1-14 ('opens only the page menu') would fail; the fallback is a recogniser delegate that refuses touches inside the page bars (review of the pages stream, minor 4; device check M1-14 decides).
  - SDK availability of UIDeferredMenuElement elementWithUncachedProvider: (iOS 15, deployment target 16.0; the stub was added from memory of the Apple header).
  - The page menu while the Conditions or Layers sheet is open at its medium detent (the page bars stay tappable) is not in the DeviceTestPack.

Found by the whole-milestone review and recorded, not changed (minor): the New Condition form takes the decimal pad for Rise / Run / Display Size % (no minus key, locale separator); a failed re-read after a committed write leaves the in-memory lists old until the bid is reopened; the Set Scale capture is O(n x m) and its save is synchronous; a stored page scale of NULL or 0 is refused with only the generic text; the memory twin of Set Scale silently keeps old positions if it ever refuses after a write (not reachable by design); a `JobStatuses` cell that is not valid UTF-8 makes every bid with a status read as locked; the 2D / 3D chips dim after a page tap (pre-existing); New Layer loses the typed text when a rule refuses the name; the whole-view long-press recogniser may compete with the page menu (a recogniser-delegate fallback is ready); `OstDatabase::open` creates an empty file for a path that does not exist (not reachable in the app); a never-called convenience variant of `renamePage...`; a stray empty `a.out` in the working tree (not committed, not in `.gitignore`). Fixed in the review: `a6dc7f9`, `3b01dc1`, `1f6bfaf`, `fb2f9bd`, `17b3a1a` (eight findings in five commits), and `87cdf8c` (the ninth fix: the folder name alerts select with selectedTextRange; found by the independent recheck of the fixes, which ACCEPTED them with no blocker and no major; see `PORTING_LOG.md`, entry 2026-11-24). Recorded residuals of that recheck, not changed: there is no committed host test of the function-try-blocks of the six layer store functions (the allocation-failure probe needs its own test binary and cannot run on MSVC Debug; it stayed on a scratch copy), and `+[UIAction actionWithHandler:]` (five uses in M1, none before) had not been compiled against the real SDK when the recheck was written (**Updated 2026-11-24 (job 8, M1):** GitHub run 45528720676 on `2eae9ed` has since compiled it, with the rest of the Objective-C(++) of M1, with no error).
