- New Condition (NC-2, NC-3): the default layer is taken from the merged list incl. template rows, so every create fails on a bid without its own `Default`; the form forces itself dirty, so Cancel of an untouched form asks to save a form whose save must fail. NC-1: the form shows Cond. No. '0', refuses it and then drops the typed number. NC-5, NC-8, NC-10: the writer accepts an empty name, a dangling type and NaN (SQLite stores NaN as NULL). **[Job 9, `d26893c`: NC-1, NC-2, NC-3, NC-5 and NC-10 are fixed on Windows by `0a24294` (NC-1: `condition_action_handler.py:619-633`; NC-2: `:601-616`; NC-3: `edit_condition_dialog.py:2105-2112`; NC-5 / NC-10: `create_condition_spec_dto.py:63-72`, `insert_condition_use_case.py:20`, the writer itself still accepts an empty name and NaN); NC-8 (`condition_operations.py:338-346`) is unchanged and still a defect; iOS ported NC-1 (no floor at 0) and matches the rest.]**
- Layers (LY-1, LY-2, LY-4, LY-5): 'Comments' can be created or renamed to and is then merged away (invisible, undeletable); a failed usage read returns an EMPTY set, so a delete clears the references of a layer that is in use (a condition silently loses its layer); a batch delete commits the earlier deletes before a refused one; a failed reference clear is non-fatal (dead in production). LY-3: two definitions of 'in use'. LY-6: a swap with a shared neighbour raises a raw scope error. **[Job 9, `d26893c`: LY-1 (`layers_dialog.py:455`, `:503`), LY-2 (`settings_reader.py:450-480`, the usage read now raises) and LY-3 (`ui_event_coordinator.py:663-686`) are fixed on Windows by `0a24294`; LY-6 changed only its message ("Failed to move layer."); LY-4 (`project_write_service.py:6954-6990`, the `break` at `:6976`) and LY-5 (`layer_operations.py:193-207`) are unchanged and still defects; iOS matches the fixed ones.]**
- Condition folders (CF-1, CF-2): the Access layer blocks a delete only for direct use (deleting Mid succeeds although Leaf holds a condition); the service blocks every ancestor but reads the loaded in-memory bid, not the file. CF-5: New Folder never checks duplicates while rename does. CF-7: failures are only logged. **[Job 9, `d26893c`: CF-1 (`condition_folder_operations.py:90-117`, `:139-159`), CF-2 (`project_write_service.py:953-970`, `:1042-1054`: the in-use check reads stored rows) and CF-7 / CF-8 (`condition_action_handler.py:1321-1334`, `:1598-1613`: failure and refresh alerts) are fixed on Windows by `0a24294`; CF-5 changed differently (a free name "New Folder (2)" without asking); CF-3, CF-4, CF-6, CF-9 to CF-12 are unchanged; iOS matches CF-1 and CF-2, kept its texts for CF-7 / CF-8 (sentences aligned) and prefills the free name for CF-5.]**
- Set Scale (SS-1, SS-2, SS-3, SS-5, SS-10): a NULL / 0 / NaN stored scale makes every Set Scale on the page fail with no repair; no overflow guard (a huge factor writes `inf` text); the dialog accepts nan and inf; the custom fields are filled with `{:g}` (6 digits), so ticking only 'apply to all' re-saves a truncated value and rescales by the truncation error; the legend is round-tripped through `xml.etree` with latin-1 (a non-latin-1 character fails the whole change). SS-6: no undo of its own for a change rewriting every coordinate. **[Job 9, `d26893c`: SS-2 (`legend_position.py:7-17`, `page_operations.py:123-125`, `:229-231`: a coordinate that rounds to 0 at three decimals is refused, with its own text `texts.py:1057-1060`), SS-3 (`set_scale_dialog.py:276-311`), SS-5 (`set_scale_dialog.py:178-179`, `:416-418`: the shortest round-trip text) and SS-10 (`legend_position.py:40-42`: `xmlcharrefreplace`) are fixed on Windows by `0a24294`; SS-1 (`page_operations.py:78-89`) and SS-6 (no undo of its own) are unchanged; iOS ported SS-2 and SS-5 in job 9 (the "no overflow guard" and "`{:g}`" statements above are history) and keeps the UTF-8 legend bytes for SS-10.]**
- Rename and delete page (RP-2, RP-7, RP-8, DP-5, DP-1): no length limit and no bid argument (any page of any single existing bid can be renamed), the row count is ignored; the last-page rule is UI-only (the writer would delete every page); the log-and-continue branch of the child-table loop is dead in production. **[Job 9, `d26893c`: RP-2 (`page.py:12`, limit 255 in code points), RP-3 (`project_write_service.py:6591-6625`), DP-2 (`:7213-7260`) and DP-5 (`:7226-7233`, the last-page rule is now in the service, over the loaded pages) are fixed on Windows by `0a24294`; RP-7 (`page_operations.py:153`), RP-8 (`mdb_writer.py:311-315`), DP-1 (`settings_operations.py:836-853`) are unchanged; iOS keeps its UTF-16 unit count and matches the rest.]**
- Committed but reported failed (CF-8, RP-3, DP-2, LY-UI-4): rename and delete return False although the change was written, when only the reload fails. **[Job 9: all four fixed on Windows by `0a24294` (`rename_condition_folder_result` `project_write_service.py:917-951`, `save_page_name` `:6591-6625`, `delete_pages_result` `:7213-7260`, `delete_layer_result` `:6945-6952`); iOS keeps its own texts.]**
- A locked bid is refused silently on the local path (NC-12, project_write_service.py:701-704), and the lock guard only knows the ACTIVE bid. **[Job 9: unchanged, now `project_write_service.py:791-794`; `active_bid_write_guard.py:7-36`.]**
- Windows tests pin several of these on purpose (marked 'pinned' in section 8 of the plan); they are kept as Windows (RP-1, RP-4, LY-12, DP-7, DP-10, DP-11, SS-13).

## Open for the owner after the two device bugs (found 2026-11-20, nothing of this was changed; the two fixes were verified on device 2026-11-21, job 8)

Verified by GitHub run 44678523124 (success): the code of the two fixes (`34e14c9`, Delete clears the selection; `aef35d8`, transparent meshes are drawn) builds against the real iPhoneOS SDK and its host tests pass (B-010); runs 44856186199 and 44953904172 (success) built and tested the code on top of it. What the fixes do on a device was not verified when this section was written; **verified on device 2026-11-21 (PASS), fixes `34e14c9` (Delete selection) and `aef35d8` (transparent meshes)** (job 8: DEV-02 and DEV-01 passed on the owner's iPhone with the IPA of run 44953904172; no notes). The points below are unchanged; the Windows-look point (an opacity of 0.5 drawn almost opaque on Windows) is a separate owner decision and stays as it is.

- **The transparent look.** The Windows renderer draws an opacity of 0.5 almost opaque: its accum and revealage shaders weight the alpha twice (renderer.cpp 647, 666, 2270, 718-719), the mesh share is about 0.997 at the fit distance (0.995 for a scene of size 3). The iOS shaders give a coverage of 1 - prod(1 - alpha), 0.5 for one layer: half see-through. Matching Windows means new fs_oit_revealage / fs_oit_composite shaders (shaderc is not available here; the headers come from `build-bgfx-ios.yml`). Computed from the Windows source, Windows not run.
- **When the camera is refit (CAM-02, CAM-03, closed decisions).** The far plane now reaches a scene that appears without a refit, but the camera still looks where it looked: if the first selected 3D page has no takeoffs the scene appears out of view until Reset. Windows frames the first non-empty scene of a bid (`mesh_view.py _initialize_camera_for_current_scene`).
- **The portrait crop of the fit.** The fit distance is 1.80278 times the size (Windows show_object, no aspect); on a phone in portrait about 39 to 47 percent of the vertices lie inside the side planes (pages of ratio 48 and more), at 4:3 all of them.
- **A condition picked in the Conditions list highlights its takeoffs as a group** (iOS only; Windows selects nothing, the sidebar only starts a place session; the "Select Objects" commands are explicit). A drag of one of them moves the group. Delete does nothing in that state, where Windows would delete the explicitly selected takeoffs.
- **Undo and redo clear without the Windows page check.** The undo of a Delete selects only while its page is on screen (like Windows `_history_selection_owner`); the redo of a Delete and the undo of a Place clear the selection whatever page is shown (Windows: only while the page that owned the step is current). The safe direction.
- **The clear after Delete drops the condition highlight also in Place mode** (the placement tool keeps its condition). iOS has one highlight field and cannot tell who owns it; Windows keeps a highlight that a placement session owns.
- **UND-10 selects one takeoff**, Windows selects every restored takeoff (the parent and its holes).
- **The depth range of an unfitted scene.** The far plane can be thousands of units while the near plane scales with the camera distance (0.06 at the start): coplanar faces may flicker until the first fit. `Mat4::perspective` builds an OpenGL style depth range although Metal clips z to 0..1 (unchanged by this job): everything nearer than about twice the near plane is clipped.
- **No on-screen diagnostic was added.** The cause of bug 1 is a definite defect found by reading the code and confirmed by every reviewer, but it is GPU behaviour that cannot be run here; if the transparent meshes are still invisible after this build, the diagnostic line (mesh count per stage, bounds, camera distance, near/far, OIT availability) is the next step. Two `NSLog` lines were added instead (an unusable OIT framebuffer, a failed scene generation).

Obsolete or historical claims (do not reuse):

- 'Metal is not available / software rasterizer is production': obsolete (bgfx on Metal).
- 'Desktop OpenGL renderer must be ported directly': obsolete (replaced by bgfx/Metal).
- 'PDFium must be built for PDF viewing': obsolete for viewing (CGPDF).
- 'MDB/pyodbc required on iOS': obsolete (SQLite plus direct import).
- 'PySide6 blocks UI': obsolete (UIKit).
- 'GLM blocks curved meshes': obsolete (no GLM use).
- 'No .xcodeproj, CI or test target': obsolete, see `BUILD.md` (a generated Xcode project, `build.yml`, `Config/Tests`).
- 'Metric measurement, quantity rounding and attachment aggregation are not implemented': obsolete (QTY-01, QTY-02, QTY-04, QTY-05, COND-METRIC-01 are fixed).
- 'Minimum iOS 14.8 / 15, Release only optimised, Nyxian compiles with -O0': obsolete (16.0 and `-Os` everywhere).
- '8 items blocked-needs-decision', 'Needs my decision' for the formal items and candidates: obsolete (all decided on 2026-11-19, section "Decided on 2026-11-19").
- 'No iOS build exists', 'the CI never ran past the unit tests', 'nothing was built or signed on macOS' (B-010 before the runs of 2026-11-20 UTC): obsolete, see B-010 for the recorded runs (seven when this was written; nine since: Updated 2026-11-24 (job 8, M1), with run 45039125909 on `main` and run 45528720676 on `ios-feature-m1`); what stays true is that the Objective-C(++) behaviour that the device checks DEV-01 to DEV-11 do not cover (the 23 fixed-ui-unverified items) has not been checked on a device (the owner ran the IPA of run 44661853681 on a phone: the two device bugs of 2026-11-20; **updated 2026-11-21 (job 8):** the IPA of run 44953904172 passed DEV-01 to DEV-11 on the owner's iPhone).
- 'Windows looks accidental' for the 12 possible defects that Windows fixed in `b9902e6` (listed in the table "Possible Windows defects"): obsolete since job 5.
- 'Windows accepts a condition number up to 64 bits': wrong (Windows caps it at 2147483647).
- 'Windows flips EVERY positive when the export holds a negative' and 'one Windows OBJ mixes both windings because of that flip': obsolete (the `b9902e6` rule is a bounding-box rule; the generator winding split of EXP-17 remains).
- 'Windows keeps control characters in names', 'Windows writes the raw name in the OBJ and MTL comment lines', 'Windows would raise on a NULL RefNo', 'Windows disagrees with itself on a stored TakeoffIncrements of 0', 'a Windows staging name taken by a directory is only reused by luck', 'a DATETIME integer beyond the C range aborts the Windows import': obsolete (fixed on Windows in `b9902e6`).
- 'iOS treats the Image layer like any other layer' and 'Hide All hides layer-less conditions on Windows': obsolete.
- 'COND-STYLE-02 and COND-ELEV-01 are deliberate deviations from Windows' and 'a style change persists the type default spacing unless edited': obsolete (job 5, code changes 1 and 2).
- The counts of 2026-11-19 and of 2026-11-20 before job 5 (172 fixed, 25 fixed-ui-unverified, 21 wont-fix, 1 no-longer-divergent; 1598 tests on 2026-11-19, 1632 before job 5): superseded by 173, 26, 16, 4 and 1720 (1729 with Manifold; superseded again in job 6 by 172, 27 and 1760 (1769), see the last bullet of this list; the 172 and the 27 were superseded in job 8 by 176 and 23, the tests stay 1760 (1769)).
- 'A stored condition type outside 0-3 shows Linear in the Edit Condition editor and a Save rewrites the type like a style change' (COND-M08, decided 2026-11-16 / 2026-11-19, kept in job 5): obsolete since job 6 (disabled "Unknown (N)" entry, the stored type is kept; `editor_condition_type` and `CondM08_UnsupportedType.*` are gone).
- 'What Windows does for the thickness of an unsupported condition type is unverified' (owner note e): obsolete since job 6 (Windows `49a2d59` has six tests for it).
- 'Windows accepts `5'6` as 60 inches and `10'6` as 120 in the Edit Condition dialog' (owner note a): obsolete for the dialog since `49a2d59` (`5'x` is still accepted; the loader still reads a stored `5'6` as 60).
- '`duplicate_condition` computes `COALESCE(MAX(RefNo),0)+1`, reads it with `column_int`, and Windows is unguarded too' (owner note g): obsolete since job 6 (guarded on both sides; only `ost_store::shift_condition_ref_nos` stays unguarded and unused).
- 'The Windows reference is `b9902e6`' and 'the datetime rule is `import_operations.py:608`': obsolete since job 6 (`49a2d59`; `ost_text_values.py:15-22`).
- The counts 173 fixed, 26 fixed-ui-unverified, 1720 tests (1729 with Manifold): superseded by 172, 27 and 1760 (1769 with Manifold); the 172 fixed and 27 fixed-ui-unverified were superseded again in job 8 by 176 and 23 (the tests stay 1760 (1769 with Manifold)).

## Windows features without an iOS counterpart (not part of this job)

The job brought the existing iOS features in line with Windows and added no feature. **Updated 2026-11-24 (job 8, M1):** milestone M1 then built New Condition, condition folders, layer create / rename / delete / reorder, Set Scale, rename page and delete page (section "M1 (2026-11-24)"); they leave this list (the rest of the page settings, default layers and condition type creation stay on it). The rows below are the state before M1 and stay as they were; they are the "Not implemented" and "Obsolete" rows of `PORTING_MAP.md`.

- Not implemented, product scope: FBX export, Summary CSV export, AI takeoff, SQL Server collaboration, detached windows, desktop business workflows (payroll, employees, job statuses, cover sheet, bid areas), annotations and hotlinks, page settings editing (Set Scale, rename and delete page; **Updated 2026-11-24 (job 8, M1): built in M1**, the plan organizer, image adjust and overlay stay), clipboard, paste and backout modes, creation of conditions, condition folders, layers and bids (**Updated 2026-11-24 (job 8, M1): New Condition, condition folders and layer create / rename / delete / reorder are built in M1**; creation of bids, default layers and condition types stay), snap to PDF lines and takeoff endpoints, 3D section cuts and the whole-scene edge overlay, overlay image drawing, bitonal/invert/adjust image modes, the tower crane model, OST/OSP and PDF export (B-004, B-009).
- Obsolete on iOS: Access/MDB, desktop licensing and updates, Windows installer, realtime sync with the OST desktop app, menus, shortcuts and toolbars, MCP helpers, SQL Server credential storage.
- **Superseded / updated 2026-11-21 (job 6):** Windows `bf6b8fc` changed the Cover Sheet save, SQL collaboration (New Folder after Reload), the path probe for unreachable PDF shares, the compact 3D control strip and docstrings of the tower crane: all of these stay without an iOS counterpart (confirmed by grep in job 6). An unreachable PDF share is an absent PDF on iOS (`fileExistsAtPath:` and `CGPDFDocumentCreateWithURL` never throw, no message bar exists). The Windows paths that allocate condition numbers (New Condition, paste or copy to another bid, Duplicate and Reassign, multi-condition Duplicate, Renumber Conditions) had no iOS counterpart at job 6; only Duplicate existed. **Updated 2026-11-24 (job 8, M1):** New Condition exists since M1 (2026-11-24) (`insert_condition`, through `allocate_condition_identities`, guarded like Duplicate); the others still have no iOS counterpart.

Corrections of job 5 (2026-11-20; "Earlier claim" is what this file or the other status documents said, "Reality" is the code, the tests and Windows `b9902e6`):

| Earlier claim | Reality |
|---|---|
| Windows flips EVERY positive when the export holds a negative (`boolean_operations.py:49-61`); one Windows OBJ mixes both windings because of that flip; the fix is the `boolean_applied` flag | `b9902e6`: a positive whose bounding box does not strictly overlap any same-type negative bounding box keeps its generator winding and only the result of a positive that may be cut is reversed (`boolean_operations.py:37-111`); the generator winding split (EXP-17) remains |
| Windows accepts any condition number the column holds, up to 64 bits (`edit_condition_dialog.py:1449-1469`) | Windows caps it at 2147483647 and refuses larger numbers with "Condition number must be between 1 and 2147483647." (`update_condition_dto.py:5-9`, `edit_condition_dialog.py:1464-1492`) |
| Windows raises on a NULL stored `RefNo` and `update_condition` returns False (COND-UPD-02) | `condition_operations.py:612` has an `is not None` guard; iOS matches |
| Windows disagrees with itself on a stored `TakeoffIncrements` of 0 (DBP-04, DBP-05) | `hierarchy_reader.py:126-130` keeps a stored 0; iOS matches |
| Windows keeps control characters in exported names and iOS drops them (EXP-12) | Windows writes `_` for each of U+0000-U+001F and U+007F (`base_exporter.py:15`, `:200`); iOS writes `_` too |
| Windows writes the raw condition name in the OBJ and MTL comment lines (EXP-16); the suggested fix replaces CR, LF and other control characters | Windows replaces CR and LF by one space each (`obj_exporter.py:9-10`, `:50`, `:74`); iOS matches |
| A Windows staging name taken by a directory is only handled by luck (IMP-12); a DATETIME integer beyond the C range aborts the import (IMP-20 (1)) | `osp_importer.py:428-430` returns False for a destination that is not a regular file; `import_operations.py:608` catches `OverflowError`; iOS matches |
| iOS treats the Image layer like any other layer (COND-PLACE-01 note 1) | iOS has the Windows Image-layer exemption on the single toggle and the tap guard (job 5) |
| Hide All hides layer-less conditions in Windows memory only (LP-22), and a hidden armed layer-less placement is a defect (COND-PLACE-01 note 2) | `project_data_service.py:675-700` keeps them visible; no longer a defect |
| Show All and Hide All write shared template rows on Windows (LP-24) | They skip them (`layer_operations.py:259-371`); iOS matches |
| COND-STYLE-02 and COND-ELEV-01 are deliberate deviations from Windows; a style change persists the type default spacing unless edited (decision (a) of 2026-11-16) | Windows fixed what they avoided and iOS follows Windows (code changes 1 and 2, job 5); the 2026-11-16 spacing decision is replaced |
| The elevation text of a metric bid is validated like a dimension (placeholder `e.g. 3200`) | One parser (`elevation_text_inches`) for the loader and the dialog; placeholder `e.g. 3.2 m`; a bare number is refused |
| 21 wont-fix, 25 fixed-ui-unverified, 172 fixed, 1 no-longer-divergent (2026-11-19); 1632 tests (1640 with Manifold) (2026-11-20, before job 5) | 16, 26, 173 and 4; 1720 tests (1729 with Manifold) |
| The code of job 5 was not yet seen by any GitHub run and its Objective-C(++) wiring was only syntax-checked against stubs (this file as pushed in `df456a2`, before run 44856186199) | GitHub run 44856186199 (success, head `df456a2`) built and tested it: it compiles and links against the real iPhoneOS 18.5 SDK and the host tests pass in 12 of 12 areas (Manifold stub); the wiring has never run on a device, so its behaviour at runtime is still unverified |
